<h3 class="section-title-small">
            ACADEMIC STORIES
        </h3>
</div>
<div class="section article-section articles-block article-section-main">
<div class="article-item">
<div class="item-inner">
<a class="academic-story__link" href="https://academicstories.com/story/science-for-humanity/our-plastic-pollution-has-created-a-whole-new-ocean-ecosystem?utm_source=Academic%20Positions&amp;utm_medium=referral&amp;utm_campaign=startpage" target="_blank">
<img alt="" class="article-image" src="https://s3.eu-central-1.amazonaws.com/ap-production-media-storage/751/conversions/mobile.jpg"/>
<div class="academic-story__info">
<h3 class="article-category article-category-green">
<p>Science for Humanity</p>
</h3>
<h4 class="job-title article-title-stories">Our Plastic Pollution Has Created a Whole New Ocean Ecosystem</h4>
<div class="article-text"><p>Did you know that the plastic we throw out has created a completely new ocean habitat? It’s called the Plastisphere.</p></div>
</div>
</a>
</div>
</div>
<div class="article-item">
<div class="item-inner">
<a class="academic-story__link" href="https://academicstories.com/story/discoveries/using-3d-printing-to-reduce-the-environmental-impact-of-desalination?utm_source=Academic%20Positions&amp;utm_medium=referral&amp;utm_campaign=startpage" target="_blank">
<img alt="" class="article-image" src="https://s3.eu-central-1.amazonaws.com/ap-production-media-storage/760/conversions/mobile.jpg"/>
<div class="academic-story__info">
<h3 class="article-category article-category-green">
<p>Discoveries</p>
</h3>
<h4 class="job-title article-title-stories">Using 3D Printing to Reduce the Environmental Impact of Desalination</h4>
<div class="article-text"><p>As the world’s desalination capacity grows, it has become increasingly important to find a way to make the process more sustainable.</p></div>
</div>
</a>
</div>
</div>
<div class="article-item">
<div class="item-inner">
<a class="academic-story__link" href="https://academicstories.com/story/discoveries/ultrasound-isn-t-just-for-babies-it-could-also-help-build-a-better-computer-chip?utm_source=Academic%20Positions&amp;utm_medium=referral&amp;utm_campaign=startpage" target="_blank">
<img alt="" class="article-image" src="https://s3.eu-central-1.amazonaws.com/ap-production-media-storage/742/conversions/mobile.jpg"/>
<div class="academic-story__info">
<h3 class="article-category article-category-green">
<p>Discoveries</p>
</h3>
<h4 class="job-title article-title-stories">Ultrasound Isn’t Just for Babies. It Could Also Help Build a Better Computer Chip.</h4>
<div class="article-text"><p>ARCNL PhD student Stephen Edwards uses a laser to launch extremely high-frequency soundwaves (ultrasound) into the layers of computer chips.</p></div>
</div>
</a>
</div>
</div>
</div>
